I would ask those ladies and gentlemen who are in favour of this supplementary or amendment to be a sign. – This is the majority and thus accepted. We shall now proceed to the vote on the remaining parts of the text of the Federal Finance Act, together with the title and receipt in 60 of the Supplements as amended by the Committee's report in 80 of the Supplements. When I agree, I'm looking for an important sign. – This is the majority and thus accepted. Finally, we are voting on the investments belonging to the Federal Finance Act, in so far as they have not yet been voted on. This is the summary according to groups and chapters of Appendix I and Appendices Ia to Ic (total summaries) taking into account the amendments resulting from the special reports in 80 of the Supplements, as well as the summaries according to groups and chapters of Appendix II – Economic Balance Estimate and its summary breakdown in Appendix IIa – and also Appendix III – establishment plan – and Annex IV – vehicle plan – as amended by the Committee Report in 60 and 60 of the Supplements. I ask those ladies and gentlemen who give their consent to this to give a sign in this regard. – I notice: This is the majority, and it is accepted. This concludes the second reading on the Bundesfinanzgesetz 2000 together with investments. We shall now proceed to the third reading. I ask those ladies and gentlemen, who are also at third reading for this draft law, to have a sign of approval. – This is the majority and thus accepted. The draft law is therefore also adopted at third reading. The budget for the year 2000 has been adopted. [[Congratulations on the ÖVP and the Freeman.]] The agenda is exhausted. Inflow
